# SAOM effects inventory: RSiena 1.6.6 versus `nwsaom`

- **Date:** 2026-10-22
- **nwsaom commit inspected:** `4c894b0 Behavior centring by the mean of the wave means of the observed values (RSiena); convergence message states the two criteria separately; avAltPop/totAltPop notes consistent` (checkout `/Users/tgrund/FILES_NEW/SOFTWARE/nwsaom`)
- **Working tree state when the table was generated:** uncommitted changes present: M .gitignore;  M CITATION.cff;  M docs/inventory_tools/gen_md.py;  M manual/ch01_intro.tex;  M manual/ch05_effects.tex;  M manual/ch11_missing.tex; D  tools/__pycache__/set_version.cpython-314.pyc
- **How the RSiena list was generated:** `Rscript` with RSiena 1.6.6 installed: `getEffects()` on `sienaDataCreate()` objects for 12 data configurations built from `s501-s503`, `s50a`, `s50s` and made-up bipartite/non-directed/continuous data (first generated by an agent 2026-10-22; the row data are `docs/inventory_tools/rows.json`): (1) one-mode network with `coCovar`, `varCovar`, `coDyadCovar`, `varDyadCovar`; (2) network + behavior with the same covariates; (3) network + two behaviors; (4) two networks + behavior; (5) three networks; (6) bipartite with node-set covariates and a dyadic covariate; (7) bipartite + behavior; (8) one-mode + bipartite on shared node sets; (9) network + continuous (SDE) behavior; (10-12) the same for symmetrised (non-directed) networks. Plus `allEffects` (1.6.6) to catch the 17 shortNames those configurations cannot instantiate (settings effects, `sameXV*`, `toU`, `BFDeg`). Result: all 456 distinct shortNames of `allEffects` appear. Rows are unique per (variable type, shortName, interaction context), with `effectName` templated (`<dep>` = dependent variable name, `<v1>`/`<v2>` = interaction variables); role sets come from the `type` column of `getEffects()` (eval / endow / creation / rate / gmm). RSiena sections follow the manual (RSiena_Manual.tex, "Mathematical definition of effects": structural, dyadic covariate, monadic covariate, multiple network, behavioral, rate), sub-divided where the manual lists one long block.
- **How nwsaom status was determined:** by reading `nwsaom.ado` (syntax line, guard lists for two-mode / symmetric / tvcov, `addterm()` blocks), `unw_saom.do` (`_SaomRSLabel1`, covariate-split helpers, behavior models), `nwsaom.sthlp`, `nwsaom_remarks.sthlp`, `native/saom_sim.c`, and `docs/nwsaom_DECISIONS.md` (decisions 1-54), `docs/nwsaom_WORKING.md`, `docs/SAOM_ROADMAP.md`. Validation flags come from those documents ("statistics identical to siena07 targets"). Stata was not run; nothing was built.
- **Version of the counts:** all counts in this inventory (1,201 rows, 80 continuous, 1,121 plannable, 1,058 implemented) come from the effects table of RSiena 1.6.6 (`getEffects()` over the twelve configurations above). Effects that RSiena 1.6.7 to 1.6.14 added or changed (for example WXZX, the two-mode toU) are implemented and compared with RSiena 1.6.14 but are not part of these counts; the counts change with RSiena releases.
- **Caveat:** RSiena 1.6.6 (installed) differs slightly from the source clone (1.6.13): the clone adds `WXZX` (not in 1.6.6). `inPop` reports internal parameter 0 in 1.6.6 `getEffects()`, `outPop`, `inAct`, `outActSqrt` report 1.
- **The develop tree is being edited by another session** (protocol 33 was committed while this inventory was being written); statuses reflect the commit above. Maintained with `docs/inventory_tools/update_inventory.py` (append the new effects to UPDATES, run it): it regenerates this file and the CSV.

Status legend: **IMPLEMENTED** (an nwsaom option gives the effect; `IMPLEMENTED (as X)` when the nwsaom option name differs from the RSiena shortName or covers it with a different parameter), **PARTIAL** (only a restricted form or a separate restricted command), **NOT IMPLEMENTED** (no option; or implemented elsewhere but refused for this variable type), **NOT PLANNED** (explicitly out of scope: continuous behavior), **UNKNOWN** (could not be determined from the code alone, reason in notes). Roles are RSiena effect types: `eval` = evaluation function, `endow` = endowment, `creation` = creation. "Validated" = documented statistic targets identical to siena07 (`yes`), `no` = no comparison recorded, `unknown`.

## 2. The 15 most commonly used RSiena effects that nwsaom still lacks (in order of importance)

Ranked by how often they appear in applied SAOM papers (RSiena manual tutorials: selection and influence analyses of friendship and behavior, multiplex and dyadic-covariate models) and by the number of models they block. Effects counted as available: everything IMPLEMENTED or PARTIAL above.

| Rank | RSiena effect(s) | Why it matters / what nwsaom has instead |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `egoX`, `altX`, `egoPlusAltX` of the dependent behavior (network selection on the co-evolving behavior: "alcohol ego", "alcohol alter") | **DONE 2026-10-22** (`behegox`, `behaltx`, `behegoplusalt`, DECISIONS 56). Still open in the family: absDiffX, diffX, higher, egoSqX, altSqX ... with the dependent behavior. |
| 2 | `effFrom` (covariate main effect on a behavior: gender, age, ... on drinking/smoking) | No covariate effects on behavior at all; the behavior side has only `linear quadratic avalt avsim`. Needed in most influence models. |
| 3 | `indeg`, `outdeg` (effect of in-/outdegree on the behavior; also `recipDeg`, `isolate`) | Network-position effects on behavior are missing. |
| 4 | `totAlt`, `totSim`, `avAltW`/`totAltW`, `avSimPopAlt`, `maxAlt`/`minAlt` (alternative influence mechanisms) | Only `avAlt` and `avSim` exist; total-alter and similarity-total variants are standard robustness checks. |
| 5 | `avXAlt`, `avSameXAlt`, `totXAlt` (influence moderated by a covariate, e.g. same-gender influence) | Covariate-moderated influence has no counterpart. |
| 6 | Cross-network effects `from`, `to`, `crprodRecip`, `crprodMutual`, `closure`, `sharedIn`, `cl.XWX` (multiplex / several dependent networks) | Only `crprod` exists, in the separate restricted subcommand `nwsaom multiplex` (outdegree + reciprocity + crprod, two waves, no covariates, no behavior). |
| 7 | `transTriads`, `degPlus` (triadic closure and degree effects for non-directed networks) | The non-directed model offers only outdegree, transTies, balance, gwesp, cycle4, inPopSqrt, outAct, outInAss, isolate effects and covariate effects; `transTrip` is refused under `symmetric` and RSiena offers `transTriads` instead. |
| 8 | Changing dyadic covariate `varDyadCovar` (`X`, `XRecip`) and changing-covariate versions of the other covariate effects (`absDiffX`, `egoXaltX`, `diffX`, `higher`, `egoSqX`, ...), `RateX` with a changing covariate, endow/creation with `tvcov()` | `tvcov()` supports only egox/altx/nodecov/samex/simx; `dyadcov()` takes one time-constant matrix. |
| 9 | Covariate x triadic/popularity effects: `sameXTransTrip`, `simXTransTrip`, `homXTransTrip`, `sameXInPop`, `sameXOutAct`, `homXOutAct`, `inPopX`, `outActX`, `altXOutAct` | Homophily-moderated closure/popularity effects are not available (none of the covariate-interaction effects of the triad/degree families). |
| 10 | Network rate effects `outRate`, `inRate`, `recipRate` (+ `Inv`, `Log` versions) and behavior `RateX` | Only a constant rate (jointly estimated) and `ratecov()` with constant node covariates exist. |
| 11 | `denseTriads` | Deliberately deferred (DECISIONS 53: marking-based statistic with five configuration tables, parameter 5/6); a frequent triad-structure alternative to transTrip. |
| 12 | Two-mode: `inPop`, `outActSqrt`, `gwespFBMix`, `sameXCycle4`, `sameXInPop`, `JoutMix`, two-mode + behavior (`bip` x `beh`), covariates other than egoX/altX | Two-mode supports 8 structural effects and egoX/altX only; behavior with a bipartite network is refused; `inpop`/`outactsqrt`/`isolatepop` pass the two-mode guard but are undocumented (UNKNOWN). |
| 13 | Behavior interactions (`includeInteraction` for behavior effects, `behUnspInt`) and cross-behavior effects (`effFrom` between behaviors, `avGroupEgoX`) | `interact()` is for network effects only; several behaviors interact only through the network (DECISIONS 28). |
| 14 | Covariate threshold/contrast effects: `egoPlusAltSqX`, `diffSqX`, `egoDiffX`, `altHigherEgoX`, `unequalX`, `egoLThresholdX`, `egoRThresholdX`, `altLThresholdX`, `altRThresholdX`, `degAbsDiffX` | Only 13 of the ~60 monadic covariate effects are available. |
| 15 | Centred / ego-form degree variants and compression/primary-setting family: `inPop.c`, `outPop.c`, `outAct.c`, `inAct.c`, `outPopMore`, `outPopSqrtMore`, `avDeg`, `Jin`, `Jout`, `primary`, `nonPCompress`, `primCompress` | Lower priority: centred variants and niche effects; the primary-setting/compression family is architecturally blocked (ROADMAP unit 174). |

Other gaps not ranked: GMoM statistics (`*_gmm`; nwsaom estimates by Method of Moments only), settings effects (`settingSizeAct` ...; no settings concept), continuous (SDE) dependent variables (NOT PLANNED), Siena Bayes / maximum likelihood (not planned, DECISIONS 49; an siena08-style meta-analysis is planned).

## 3. Verification of IMPLEMENTED claims by grep in the develop tree

For each nwsaom option named below (75 options, all claims of IMPLEMENTED/PARTIAL rows), the number of hits in the dev tree: `syntax` = occurrences in the `syntax [...]` option list of `nwsaom.ado` (lines 191-215), `ado+mata` = occurrences of the quoted term / `stat_saom_X` / `change_saom_X` / `addterm` references in `nwsaom.ado` and `unw_saom.do`, `help` = `{opt X}` entries in `nwsaom.sthlp`, `C` = case-insensitive occurrences in `native/saom_sim.c` (native termcode). A zero in one column is explained where relevant (`sqrtcycle4` registers the term `cycle4sqrt` with `stat_saom_cycle4sqrt`; `dblnbrdist2` registers term `nbrdist2twice`; `ratecov`/`tvcov` are handled by dedicated code paths without `addterm`; `egox`/`altx`/`samex`/`simx` are aliases of nodeocov/nodeicov/nodematch/simcov; `indegpopularity`/`outpopularity`/`inplus3` use shorter C names).

Additional manual checks: the two-mode refusal list (nwsaom.ado lines ~544-551) and the symmetric refusal list (~1087-1091) were read to assign the two-mode and non-directed statuses; `_SaomRSLabel1` in `unw_saom.do` was read for the RSiena effect names nwsaom reports in `e(rsiena_labels)`.

## 4. Other unresolved points

- Two-mode: `inpop`, `outactsqrt` and `isolatepop` are not in the two-mode refusal list nor documented as available; they were not tested (Stata not run) and are marked UNKNOWN.
- Non-directed networks: only the `outdegree` estimates were compared with siena07 (BJOINT); the other non-directed rows say `unknown` for validation although the effects are shared code with the directed model.
- `outPopSqrt` / `inActSqrt` deliberately follow the documented sqrt statistic (= RSiena with parameter 1), not the RSiena 1.6.6 default (parameter 0), see RSiena issue #151 in docs/SAOM_ROADMAP.md.
- The RSiena `effectName` column shows one representative spelling with `<dep>`, `<v1>`, `<v2>` placeholders; some effects print slightly different names depending on the number of waves/variables.
- Role columns for RSiena are what `getEffects()` lists; for some effects RSiena lists endow/creation rows even though they are rarely meaningful.

## 5. Tables by manual section

Columns: shortName | effectName (as printed) | variable type | covariate/network context | RSiena roles | internal parameter (default) | nwsaom status | nwsaom option | roles in nwsaom | validated | notes.

### A. One-mode, non-directed and two-mode network: density and reciprocity

| shortName | effectName | variable type | context | RSiena roles | parameter | nwsaom status | nwsaom option | nwsaom roles | validated | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `density` | <dep>: outdegree (density) | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED (as outdegree)** | outdegree | eval | yes | Required in every model unless outdegreeendow/outdegreecreation are used. Endow/creation split is refused: lost + gained ties equal the rate distance statistic, so not identified with an estimated rate (DECISIONS 3, 13). Unconditional/conditional estimation both available. |
| `recip` | <dep>: reciprocity | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED (as reciprocity)** | reciprocity | eval, endow, creation | yes | Split via endow(reciprocity) creation(reciprocity) or reciprocityendow/reciprocitycreation; works with waves(), present(), missnet(), behavior(). |
| `density` | <dep>: outdegree (density) | two-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED (as outdegree)** | outdegree | eval | yes | Two-mode network (nwset, bipartite; actormode()): actors choose ties to events; eval only (endow/creation refused). Statistic identical to RSiena target on the simulated 40x20 data (DECISIONS 29). |
| `density` | <dep>: degree (density) | non-directed network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED (as outdegree)** | outdegree | eval | yes | Non-directed model (symmetric option, or automatic for tie-symmetric waves): eval only (endow/creation refused with symmetric). Model types forcing/confirmation/force/agree/joint via symtype(). Required in every model unless outdegreeendow/outdegreecreation are used. Endow/creation split is refused: lost + gained ties equal the rate distance statistic, so not identified with an estimated rate (DECISIONS 3, 13). Unconditional/conditional estimation both available. |

| shortName | effectName | variable type | context | RSiena roles | parameter | nwsaom status | nwsaom option | nwsaom roles | validated | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `transTrip` | <dep>: transitive triplets | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED** | transtrip | eval, endow, creation | yes | Statistic and endow/creation targets identical to siena07 (DECISIONS 19). |
| `transTrip1` | <dep>: transitive triplets (1) | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED (as ownx(transtrip1))** | ownx(transtrip1) | eval | yes | plugin protocol 54 native only; statistics and estimates equal siena07 (s50) (DECISIONS 82). |
| `transTrip2` | <dep>: transitive triplets (2) | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED (as ownx(transtrip2))** | ownx(transtrip2) | eval | yes | plugin protocol 54 native only; statistics and estimates equal siena07 (s50) (DECISIONS 82). |
| `transMedTrip` | <dep>: transitive mediated triplets | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED** | transmedtrip | eval, endow, creation | yes | Own RSiena class TransitiveMediatedTriplets; targets identical (DECISIONS 19). |
| `transRecTrip` | <dep>: transitive recipr. triplets | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED** | transrectrip | eval, endow, creation | yes | Sparse effect: phase-1 sample size / per-period Jacobian issues documented in DECISIONS 19, 21, 27; ~1/50 seeds diverge in the split model vs 3/200 in RSiena. |
| `transRecTrip2` | <dep>: trans. recipr. triplets type 2 | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED (as ownx(transrectrip2))** | ownx(transrectrip2) | eval | yes | plugin protocol 54 native only; statistics and estimates equal siena07 (s50) (DECISIONS 82). |
| `cycle3` | <dep>: 3-cycles | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED** | cycle3 | eval, endow, creation | yes |  |
| `transTies` | <dep>: transitive ties | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED** | transties | eval, endow, creation | yes | General lost/gained-ties mechanism built here first (DECISIONS 6). |
| `between` | <dep>: betweenness | one-mode network |  | eval | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED** | between | eval | yes | Copied exactly from RSiena including that the simulation contribution omits the paths i->k->j the new tie closes (DECISIONS 53, protocol 32). Refused with symmetric and two-mode. |
| `balance` | <dep>: balance | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED** | balance | eval, endow, creation | yes | balanceMean computed from the data as RSiena (calcBalmean); missing-network-data aware (DECISIONS 25). Residual approximation: union (start OR end) missing list in the simulated endow/creation statistic. |
| `nbrDist2` | <dep>: number of actors at dist 2 | one-mode network |  | eval | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED** | nbrdist2 | eval | yes | Copied exactly from RSiena (its simulation contribution only follows the ego row); statistic identical to siena07 on s50 (DECISIONS 54, protocol 33). Eval only; refused with symmetric and two-mode. |
| `nbrDist2twice` | <dep>: number pairs at doubly achieved dist 2 | one-mode network |  | eval | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED (as dblnbrdist2)** | dblnbrdist2 | eval | yes | Option is dblnbrdist2 (term nbrdist2twice) because Stata syntax rejects an option name with a digit followed by letters. At least two two-paths; statistic identical to siena07 (DECISIONS 54). Refused with symmetric and two-mode. |

| `gwespFF` | <dep>: GWESP I -> K -> J (#) | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | yes: internal parameter, default 69 | **IMPLEMENTED (as gwesp(#))** | gwesp(#) | eval, endow, creation | yes | nwsaom takes the DECAY directly: RSiena gwespFF parameter 69 = gwesp(.69). With endow()/creation() the number gives the decay and the effect is split. Mata and native. |
| `gwespBB` | <dep>: GWESP I <- K <- J (#) | one-mode network |  | eval, endow, creation | yes: internal parameter, default 69 | **IMPLEMENTED** | gwespbb(#) | eval | yes | Decay given directly (RSiena parameter / 100, e.g. 0.69); statistic identical to siena07 on s50, estimates within noise over 3 seeds (DECISIONS 54, protocol 33). | `from.w.ind` | <dep>: from <v1> agr. weighted by <v2> ind | one-mode network | another network + another network | eval, endow, creation | internal parameter, default 1 | **IMPLEMENTED (as cross1)** | cross1(fromwindoo | eval | yes | fromwindow / fromwindwo) / new (codes 55-57): the agreement network / the in-degree weight network = other,other / other,own / own,other. ### P. Behavior: shape effects

| shortName | effectName | variable type | context | RSiena roles | parameter | nwsaom status | nwsaom option | nwsaom roles | validated | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `linear` | <dep> linear shape | behavior |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED** | linear | eval | yes | Required with behavior() unless linearendow+linearcreation; the endow/creation split of linear itself is refused (decreases + increases = behavior-rate distance statistic, not identified). Not available with a two-mode network (behavior() is refused there). With a non-directed network (symmetric + behavior) available, only partly validated (DECISIONS 14, 24, 26: simulation recovery with RSiena BJOINT; RSiena own fit unstable). |
| `quad` | <dep> quadratic shape | behavior |  | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED (as quadratic)** | quadratic | eval, endow, creation | yes | quadraticendow/quadraticcreation (or endow(quadratic) creation(quadratic)) allowed together with linear. Not available with a two-mode network (behavior() is refused there). With a non-directed network (symmetric + behavior) available, only partly validated (DECISIONS 14, 24, 26: simulation recovery with RSiena BJOINT; RSiena own fit unstable). |

| shortName | effectName | variable type | context | RSiena roles | parameter | nwsaom status | nwsaom option | nwsaom roles | validated | notes |

| `avGroupEgoX` | <v1> group-av. ego (net2) | behavior | a behavior variable | eval, endow, creation | no (default 0) | **IMPLEMENTED (as this row is the network effect avGroupEgoX with the dependen)** | this row is the network effect avGroupEgoX with the dependent behavior as covariate in the SECOND network of nwsaom multinet: eff2(outdegree reciprocity netbeh:avgroupegox) (eff1() for the first) | eval | yes | stat -55.6533 (adv; -55.653); est siena07 -0.488/-0.473/-0.460 (SE 0.56) vs nwsaom -0.451/-0.450/-0.491 (SE 0.54-0.58) |

(The two group effects together are collinear, siena07 SE = NA.) A single-network bug was found and fixed on the way: netbeh(totgroupegox) used the parameter xparam(1) as the number of actors, so its coefficient was n = 50 times siena07's; SaomNbSim() now returns 0 (= n actors). |
